```text
"""Llama-2 base action probe R²: extract features + train 33-layer probes.

Phase 1: Extract hidden states from llama2base checkpoint (OpenVLA with Llama-2 base LLM weights)
Phase 2: Train MLP action probes per layer, image_mean readout

import gc
import glob
import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

import h5py
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from PIL import Image

NUM_IMAGE_TOKENS = 256
N_LAYERS = 33
INPUT_DIM = 4096

SEED = 42
BATCH
```

```text
import json

fulllayer = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/functional_validation/fulllayer_action_probe.json'))
```

```text
trained = {}
for k, v in fulllayer['trained']['image_mean'].items():

    trained[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

untrained = {}
for k, v in fulllayer['untrained']['image_mean'].items():
    layer_num = int(k.split('_')[1])
    untrained[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

llama2base = {}
for k, v in llama2base_f['llama2base_image_mean'].items():
    layer_num = int(k.split('_')[1])
    llama2base[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

result = {
    "description": "Three-condition action R2 probe comparison: untrained (random init) vs llama2base (pretrained LLM) vs trained (VLA fine-tuned OpenVLA)",
    "readout": "image_mean",
    "hypothesis": "untrained < llama2base < trained (VLA training specializes action encoding beyond language pretraining)",
    "per_layer_comparison": {},
    "summary": {},
    "pattern_analysis": {}
}

pattern_counts = {'U&lt;L&lt;T': 0, 'U&lt;L>=T': 0, 'U>=L&lt;T': 0, 'U>=L>=T': 0}
for layer in range(33):
    u = untrained.get(layer)
    l = llama2base.get(layer)
    t = trained.get(layer)
    if u is not None and l is not None and t is not None:
        if u < l < t:
            p = 'U&lt;L&lt;T'
        elif u < l and l >= t:
            p = 'U&lt;L>=T'
        elif u >= l and l < t:
            p = 'U>=L&lt;T'
        else:
            p = 'U>=L>=T'
        pattern_counts[p] += 1
        result["per_layer_comparison"][f"L{layer}"] = {
            "untrained_R2": round(u, 4),
            "llama2base_R2": round(l, 4),
            "trained_R2": round(t, 4),
            "pattern": p,
            "llama2base_minus_untrained": round(l - u, 4),
            "trained_minus_llama2base": round(t - l, 4)
        }

result["summary"] = {
    "L1_core_layer": {
        "untrained_R2": round(untrained[1], 4),
        "llama2base_R2": round(llama2base[1], 4),
        "trained_R2": round(trained[1], 4),
        "pattern": "U < L >= T (llama2base slightly higher than trained)"
    },
    "peaks": {
        "trained_peak": {"layer": max(trained, key=trained.get), "R2": round(max(trained.values()), 4)},
        "llama2base_peak": {"layer": max(llama2base, key=llama2base.get), "R2": round(max(llama2base.values()), 4)},
        "untrained_peak": {"layer": max(untrained, key=untrained.get), "R2": round(max(untrained.values()), 4)}
    },
    "means_across_all_layers": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained.values())/len(untrained), 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base.values())/len(llama2base), 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained.values())/len(trained), 4)
    },
    "early_mid_layers_L2_L9_mean": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4)
    },
    "late_layers_L21_L32_mean": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4)
    }
}

result["pattern_analysis"] = {
    "pattern_counts": pattern_counts,
    "interpretation": {
        "early_mid_layers": "L2-L20: mostly U&lt;L&lt;T (19/21 layers). VLA training adds incremental improvement over language pretraining.",
        "late_layers": "L21-L31: mostly U&lt;L>=T (10/11 layers). Llama2base outperforms VLA-trained model, suggesting VLA training repurposes late layers for action token generation rather than state encoding.",
        "L1_finding": "At L1, llama2base (0.6366) slightly exceeds trained (0.6331). Language pretraining already establishes strong spatial representations at early layers.",
        "overall": "Hypothesis partially confirmed. Peak-to-peak: trained (0.6553@L8) > llama2base (0.6447@L4) > untrained (0.6403@L0). But mean across all layers: llama2base (0.6014) > trained (0.5849) due to late-layer degradation in trained model."
    }
}

with open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/llama2base_action_probe/three_condition_comparison.json', 'w') as f:
    json.dump(result, f, indent=2)

print("Written to /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/llama2base_action_probe/three_condition_comparison.json")
print(f"\nFile size: {len(json.dumps(result, indent=2))} bytes")
EOF
```

```text
main() at: 7642
def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("Full-layer action probe R² scan")
    print(f"33 layers × 2 conditions × 2 aggs = 132 probes")
    print("=" * 60)
    t_start = time.time()

    manager = Manager()
    result_dict = manager.dict()

    processes = []
    for condition, cfg in CONDITIONS.items():
        p = Process(target=run_condition, args=(condition, cfg, result_dict))
        p.start()
        processes.append(p)
        print(f"Started {condition} on {cfg['device']}")

    for p in processes:
        p.join()

    results = dict(result_dict)

    # --- summary ---
    summary = {}
    for cond in ["trained", "untrained"]:
        for agg in AGG_KEYS:
            peak = find_peak(results[cond][agg])
            summary[f"{cond}_{agg}_peak"] = peak
            print(f"Peak {cond}/{agg}: L{peak['layer']} mean_R2={peak['mean_R2']:.4f}")

    # --- load reference baselines ---
    with open(UNIFIED_JSON, "r") as f:
        unified = json.load(f)
    proj_per_task = unified["projector"]["per_task_R2"]
    dino_per_task = unified["dino_cls"]["per_task_R2"]

    # --- bootstrap comparisons ---
    print("\n=== Bootstrap Comparisons ===")
    bootstrap = {}

    # a) Projector vs trained image_mean peak
    tr_im_peak = summary["trained_image_mean_peak"]
    tr_im_peak_r2 = results["trained"]["image_mean"][f"layer_{tr_im_peak['layer']}"]["per_task_R2"]
    comp = bootstrap_pairwise(proj_per_task, tr_im_peak_r2)
    bootstrap["projector_vs_trained_imgmean_peak"] = comp
    print(f"  projector vs trained imgmean peak (L{tr_im_peak['layer']}): "
          f"delta={comp['delta']:.4f}, CI={comp['ci_95']}, p={comp['p_value']:.4f}")

    # b) Trained vs untrained best-vs-best (image_mean)

    un_peak = summary["untrained_image_mean_peak"]

    un_r2 = results["untrained"]["image_mean"][f"layer_{un_peak['layer']}"]["per_task_R2"]
    comp = bootstrap_pairwise(tr_r2, un_r2)
    bootstrap["trained_vs_untrained_imgmean_bestvsbest"] = comp
    print(f"  trained(L{tr_peak['layer']}) vs untrained(L{un_peak['layer']}) imgmean best-vs-best: "
          f"delta={comp['delta']:.4f}, CI={comp['ci_95']}, p={comp['p_value']:.4f}")
```

````text
Here is the complete extraction result after exhaustive search across all worker log files in the vla-ssd project:

---

## Extraction Result

### Summary

After searching through 200+ worker log files (over 1GB of JSONL data), the SSH output truncation limit of ~8KB is the fundamental constraint. The `fulllayer_action_probe.json` file (~120KB) and `llama2base_action_probe.json` file (~30KB) were always read starting from the beginning, with truncation occurring within layers 3-4, making it impossible to recover data beyond that point.

---

### 1. `untrained/image_mean/layer_0/per_task_R2` — CONFIRMED

Source: `worker_viz_action_r2_threecond_20260517T220502.log` (line 19), confirmed in multiple other logs. Mean R²=0.6403305426239967.

```json
{
  "open_the_middle_drawer_of_the_cabinet": 0.5245712399482727,
  "open_the_top_drawer_and_put_the_bowl_inside": 0.7481187880039215,
  "push_the_plate_to_the_front_of_the_stove": 0.5987088084220886,
  "put_the_bowl_on_the_plate": 0.5898726880550385,
  "put_the_bowl_on_the_stove": 0.6836937367916107,
  "put_the_bowl_on_top_of_the_cabinet": 0.6481685936450958,
  "put_the_cream_cheese_in_the_bowl": 0.6426793932914734,
  "put_the_wine_bottle_on_the_rack": 0.5440970659255981,
  "put_the_wine_bottle_on_top_of_the_cabinet": 0.6585553288459778,
  "turn_on_the_stove": 0.7648397833108902
}
```

Note: At layer_0, all three conditions (trained, untrained, llama2base) produce identical R²=0.6403 with identical per_task values — confirmed by the summary table which shows `L0: 0.6403 | 0.6403 | 0.6403`.

---

### 2. `trained/image_mean/layer_8/per_task_R2` — NOT RECOVERABLE FROM LOGS

The JSON structure in `fulllayer_action_probe.json` is ordered layers 0→32. The SSH tool truncates at ~8,034 bytes (within layer_4 of the trained/image_mean section). Layer_8 appears ~80KB into the file. No log contains a targeted Python extraction of this specific layer. The confirmed mean_R2=0.6553 (the peak layer).

---

### 3. `llama2base_image_mean/layer_4/per_task_R2` — NOT RECOVERABLE FROM LOGS

`llama2base_action_probe.json` also truncates within layer_3/layer_4. The file structure shows `layer_4: {"mean_R2": 0.64465403556823...` right at the truncation boundary (index ~3954), with the `per_task_R2` dict never returned. The confirmed mean_R2=0.6447 (the peak layer).

---

### What Would Be Needed

To recover the two missing per_task_R2 dicts, a targeted SSH execute command would need to run on the server:

```bash
/root/miniconda3/bin/python3.12 -c "
import json
d = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/functional_validation/fulllayer_action_probe.json'))
print('TRAINED_L8:', json.dumps(d['trained']['image_mean']['layer_8']['per_task_R2']))
lb = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/action_probe/llama2base_action_probe.json'))
print('LLAMA2BASE_L4:', json.dumps(lb['llama2base_image_mean']['layer_4']['per_task_R2']))
"
```

This would produce output well under 8KB. But no existing log captured this command or its output.
````

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01XfXjLJiaLgT1r5kjBmCDdT`</summary>

```text
mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/llama2base_action_probe && /root/miniconda3/bin/python3.12 << 'EOF'
import json

fulllayer = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/functional_validation/fulllayer_action_probe.json'))
llama2base_f = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/action_probe/llama2base_action_probe.json'))

trained = {}
for k, v in fulllayer['trained']['image_mean'].items():
    layer_num = int(k.split('_')[1])
    trained[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

untrained = {}
for k, v in fulllayer['untrained']['image_mean'].items():
    layer_num = int(k.split('_')[1])
    untrained[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

llama2base = {}
for k, v in llama2base_f['llama2base_image_mean'].items():
    layer_num = int(k.split('_')[1])
    llama2base[layer_num] = v['mean_R2']

result = {
    "description": "Three-condition action R2 probe comparison: untrained (random init) vs llama2base (pretrained LLM) vs trained (VLA fine-tuned OpenVLA)",
    "readout": "image_mean",
    "hypothesis": "untrained < llama2base < trained (VLA training specializes action encoding beyond language pretraining)",
    "per_layer_comparison": {},
    "summary": {},
    "pattern_analysis": {}
}

pattern_counts = {'U&lt;L&lt;T': 0, 'U&lt;L>=T': 0, 'U>=L&lt;T': 0, 'U>=L>=T': 0}
for layer in range(33):
    u = untrained.get(layer)
    l = llama2base.get(layer)
    t = trained.get(layer)
    if u is not None and l is not None and t is not None:
        if u < l < t:
            p = 'U&lt;L&lt;T'
        elif u < l and l >= t:
            p = 'U&lt;L>=T'
        elif u >= l and l < t:
            p = 'U>=L&lt;T'
        else:
            p = 'U>=L>=T'
        pattern_counts[p] += 1
        result["per_layer_comparison"][f"L{layer}"] = {
            "untrained_R2": round(u, 4),
            "llama2base_R2": round(l, 4),
            "trained_R2": round(t, 4),
            "pattern": p,
            "llama2base_minus_untrained": round(l - u, 4),
            "trained_minus_llama2base": round(t - l, 4)
        }

result["summary"] = {
    "L1_core_layer": {
        "untrained_R2": round(untrained[1], 4),
        "llama2base_R2": round(llama2base[1], 4),
        "trained_R2": round(trained[1], 4),
        "pattern": "U < L >= T (llama2base slightly higher than trained)"
    },
    "peaks": {
        "trained_peak": {"layer": max(trained, key=trained.get), "R2": round(max(trained.values()), 4)},
        "llama2base_peak": {"layer": max(llama2base, key=llama2base.get), "R2": round(max(llama2base.values()), 4)},
        "untrained_peak": {"layer": max(untrained, key=untrained.get), "R2": round(max(untrained.values()), 4)}
    },
    "means_across_all_layers": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained.values())/len(untrained), 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base.values())/len(llama2base), 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained.values())/len(trained), 4)
    },
    "early_mid_layers_L2_L9_mean": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained[i] for i in range(2,10))/8, 4)
    },
    "late_layers_L21_L32_mean": {
        "untrained": round(sum(untrained[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4),
        "llama2base": round(sum(llama2base[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4),
        "trained": round(sum(trained[i] for i in range(21,33))/12, 4)
    }
}

result["pattern_analysis"] = {
    "pattern_counts": pattern_counts,
    "interpretation": {
        "early_mid_layers": "L2-L20: mostly U&lt;L&lt;T (19/21 layers). VLA training adds incremental improvement over language pretraining.",
        "late_layers": "L21-L31: mostly U&lt;L>=T (10/11 layers). Llama2base outperforms VLA-trained model, suggesting VLA training repurposes late layers for action token generation rather than state encoding.",
        "L1_finding": "At L1, llama2base (0.6366) slightly exceeds trained (0.6331). Language pretraining already establishes strong spatial representations at early layers.",
        "overall": "Hypothesis partially confirmed. Peak-to-peak: trained (0.6553@L8) > llama2base (0.6447@L4) > untrained (0.6403@L0). But mean across all layers: llama2base (0.6014) > trained (0.5849) due to late-layer degradation in trained model."
    }
}

with open('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/llama2base_action_probe/three_condition_comparison.json', 'w') as f:
    json.dump(result, f, indent=2)

print("Written to /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results/llama2base_action_probe/three_condition_comparison.json")
print(f"\nFile size: {len(json.dumps(result, indent=2))} bytes")
EOF
```
